# 第三方许可清单

产品：Veil 3.0.2
生成命令：`pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/third_party_notices.ps1`
校验命令：`pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/third_party_notices.ps1 -Check`

本文件由脚本根据 `cargo metadata --locked --format-version 1` 与仓库内嵌资产清单自动生成，请勿手改。发布门禁以 `-Check` 校验其是否与当前依赖一致。

对于 SPDX OR 表达式，本项目选择白名单内的宽松许可；未选择的许可不构成适用条款。

- Rust 包（含 workspace 成员）：364
- 内嵌第三方资产：3（Mermaid、linkedom、QuickJS）
- 包根目录找到许可证全文的包：319
- 仅在子目录（递归至 3 层）找到全文的包：1
- 由 manifest license_file 指定的全文：0
- 由 workspace 根 LICENSE 覆盖的包：4
- 无独立许可证文件的包（按 SPDX 标准全文与元数据归属补全）：40
- 许可证全文条目（本地文件按内容 SHA-256 去重）：203
- 按 SPDX 标准全文补全的许可证组：3

## 内嵌第三方资产

以下资产随 `veil.exe` 分发，但不通过 Cargo 依赖引入。版权行一律引自可核验的随包来源；来源中没有版权行时明确写出，不代填版权人。

### Mermaid 11.12.0

- 许可证：MIT
- 来源：`crates/veil-mermaid-offline/assets/mermaid.min.js`
- 版本来源：取自打包文件内的 `name:"mermaid",version:"..."` 字段
- 产物内许可声明（原文引自该文件末尾的 `Bundled license information` 注释块；仅将包裹 URL 的尖括号改为圆括号，其余文字未改动）：

~~~text
/*! Bundled license information:

dompurify/dist/purify.es.mjs:
  (*! @license DOMPurify 3.2.6 | (c) Cure53 and other contributors | Released under the Apache license 2.0 and Mozilla Public License 2.0 | github.com/cure53/DOMPurify/blob/3.2.6/LICENSE *)

js-yaml/dist/js-yaml.mjs:
  (*! js-yaml 4.1.0 https://github.com/nodeca/js-yaml @license MIT *)

lodash-es/lodash.js:
  (**
   * @license
   * Lodash (Custom Build) https://lodash.com/
   * Build: `lodash modularize exports="es" -o ./`
   * Copyright OpenJS Foundation and other contributors https://openjsf.org/
   * Released under MIT license https://lodash.com/license
   * Based on Underscore.js 1.8.3 http://underscorejs.org/LICENSE
   * Copyright Jeremy Ashkenas, DocumentCloud and Investigative Reporters & Editors
   *)

cytoscape/dist/cytoscape.esm.mjs:
  (*!
  Embeddable Minimum Strictly-Compliant Promises/A+ 1.1.1 Thenable
  Copyright (c) 2013-2014 Ralf S. Engelschall (http://engelschall.com)
  Licensed under The MIT License (http://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)
  *)
  (*!
  Event object based on jQuery events, MIT license
  
  https://jquery.org/license/
  https://tldrlegal.com/license/mit-license
  https://github.com/jquery/jquery/blob/master/src/event.js
  *)
  (*! Bezier curve function generator. Copyright Gaetan Renaudeau. MIT License: http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MIT_License *)
  (*! Runge-Kutta spring physics function generator. Adapted from Framer.js, copyright Koen Bok. MIT License: http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MIT_License *)
*/
~~~

- Mermaid 自身版权行：上游未在随仓库产物中附带版权行；适用 MIT，版权归属以上游发布为准（https://github.com/mermaid-js/mermaid）。

### linkedom（bundled）

- 许可证：MIT
- 来源：`crates/veil-mermaid-offline/assets/linkedom.worker.mjs`（构建时由 `build.rs` 转换为经典脚本）
- 版本：bundled（打包产物未记录上游版本号；以上游项目发布记录为准）
- 版权行：上游未在随仓库产物中附带版权行；适用 MIT，版权归属以上游发布为准（https://github.com/WebReflection/linkedom）。

### QuickJS（bundled）

- 许可证：MIT
- 来源：经 rquickjs-sys 0.9.0 捆绑；QuickJS 源码经 Rust 依赖 `rquickjs` 编译进 `veil.exe`
- 版本：bundled（由 rquickjs-sys 随 crate 固定，具体版本见 Cargo.lock）
- `rquickjs-sys` crate 元数据：
  - 版本：0.9.0
  - 许可证：MIT
  - repository：https://github.com/DelSkayn/rquickjs.git
  - authors：Mees Delzenne (mees.delzenne@gmail.com)
- 版权行（原文引自 `rquickjs-sys 0.9.0/quickjs/LICENSE`（cargo registry 源目录））：

  Copyright (c) 2017-2021 Fabrice Bellard
  Copyright (c) 2017-2021 Charlie Gordon
  Copyright (c) 2023 Ben Noordhuis
  Copyright (c) 2023 Saúl Ibarra Corretgé

- 该 crate 以 MIT 分发，捆绑 QuickJS；上述 QuickJS 版权行来自其内置 LICENSE。
- 上游项目：https://bellard.org/quickjs/

## 许可证全文

以下文本来自各包 crate 源目录中的 `LICENSE*`、`LICENCE*`、`COPYING*`、`NOTICE*`、`UNLICENSE*` 文件（优先包根目录，其次最多 3 层子目录，跳过 `target/`）；按内容 SHA-256 去重并按哈希值排序，每份文本前列出适用包与来源相对路径。引用文本中的尖括号定界符统一改为圆括号，括号内文字保持原样。

# Code

ttf-parser
Code from ttf-parser was used/adapted for the following parts:
- Reader, Writer, LazyArray16 and LazyArray16Iter
- `name` table parsing.
- `hmtx` table parsing.
- Most of the `cff` parsing logic as well auxiliary structs and functions
such as `calc_subroutine_bias`, `SIDMetadata`, `CIDMetadata` `RealNumber`,
`Index`, `IndexSize`, `OffsetSize`, `FDSelect`, `ArgumentStack`, `Top Dict` and `FONT Dict`,
`DictionaryParser`.

### f7b20c3e6d0efbbb9318e7511ac682a9e73d6e2c17541c80cab2ad0627033ca7

适用包：
- `latex-rust` 3.0.3 (`NOTICE`)

~~~text
LaTeX-Rust
Copyright 2026 Jeffrey S Carr

Licensed under MIT OR Apache-2.0.

This product embeds STIX Two Math (version 2.13), licensed under the
SIL Open Font License 1.1. See fonts/stix-two-math/OFL.txt.

STIX Fonts is a trademark of The Institute of Electrical and
Electronics Engineers, Inc.

This product includes a modified copy of the dvips color names
(dvipsnam.def), taken from the `%(*dvipsnames)` section of drivers.dtx
in the LaTeX graphics bundle.

Copyright 1994 David Carlisle and Sebastian Rahtz
Copyright 1995-1999 David Carlisle
Copyright 2000-2026 The LaTeX Project

That material is licensed under the LaTeX Project Public License,
version 1.3c or any later version. The latest version of the license
is at https://www.latex-project.org/lppl.txt and version 1.3c or later
is part of all distributions of LaTeX version 2008 or later.

data/dvipsnames.tsv is the modified file. It is clearly marked as
changed in its own header: the definitions were rewritten as a
tab-separated table and the rows were re-sorted alphabetically by
name. Upstream dvipsnam.def keeps the dvips color-wheel order. The
CMYK numbers, including TealBlue (0.86, 0, 0.34, 0.02), match
drivers.dtx. The file is named dvipsnames.tsv so it is not a drop-in
replacement for dvipsnam.def under LaTeX. The original authors and
The LaTeX Project do not maintain or support this file.

A complete unmodified copy of the graphics bundle, including
drivers.dtx, is available from
https://github.com/latex3/latex2e/tree/develop/required/graphics
(CTAN: macros/latex/required/graphics).

The MIT and Apache-2.0 terms apply to the Rust source. They do not
relicense data/dvipsnames.tsv or the STIX font. Anyone who distributes
the color table must keep the header in data/dvipsnames.tsv and this
notice, so the file stays identified as a modified work and a recipient
can still obtain the original graphics bundle.
~~~

## 无独立许可证文件的包（按 SPDX 标准全文补全）

以下上游包未在 crate 源中附带独立许可证文件；SPDX `OR` 表达式先按宽松许可优先级选定一个适用许可再分组，各包完整表达式见列表，版权归属见包元数据 authors/repository。每个分组附所选许可证的标准全文。authors 为空时记为“未声明”；authors 中的邮箱尖括号和正文中的尖括号定界符在本文件中以圆括号呈现。

### Apache-2.0

以下上游包未在 crate 源中附带独立许可证文件；适用 Apache-2.0（各包完整 SPDX 表达式见列表），版权归属见包元数据 authors/repository。
